# Agents · P · part 6

925 entries, persona-paul_graham to php-developer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **persona-paul_graham**: Avatar of Paul Graham (paul_graham) for simulated debates and meetings. Co-founder of Y Combinator, essayist, programmer; arch and donnish in tone, essayistic in argument, anchored in YC anecdote. ([zirubak/persona-studio](https://github.com/zirubak/persona-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-paul_graham.md) · ★8)
- **persona-perspective-reviewer**: Reviews a product, UI, workflow, or feature from one assigned persona perspective with evidence and measurement. ([tyroneross/ai-persona-lab](https://github.com/tyroneross/ai-persona-lab/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-perspective-reviewer.md))
- **persona-pipeline**: Run a 6-stage virtual review board (Analyze → Criticize → Suggest → Validate → Audit → Resolve) with role-typed universal personas (Tech-Lead, UX, SecOps, Privacy, QA, Critic). Pipeline depth scales with PR risk profile. Returns autonomy_score-bound recommendation. The vertical-depth verify primitive of the Convergence Engine (skills/convergence-engine) — the independent verifier that satisfies the verifier&gt;generator master condition. ([eko-im/multi-agent-os](https://github.com/eko-im/multi-agent-os/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-pipeline.md) · 2 copies)

- **persona-power-user**: 「キーボード派のパワーユーザー」ペルソナ。ショートカット / コマンドパレット / タブ操作 / エディタ入力 / ランダム連打 (fuzz) を中心に探索ハーネスを回し、ショートカット不動作・フォーカス喪失・タブ状態のズレ・連打時の競合を根拠付きで報告する安価なワーカー。Use as a persona worker in the persona-explore workflow. ([nigoh/Juml](https://github.com/nigoh/Juml/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/persona-power-user.md))

Returns a structured report with narrative summary and evidence table. Does not interact with the user. - **persona-researcher**: Works out what a website is for, picks the five reader personas that matter most for that kind of site (recruiters for a portfolio, buyers and evaluators for SaaS, shoppers for a store, and so on), researches each persona on the web with sources, and writes a profile and a ready-to-run simulated-reader agent for each. Use during a deep-redesign intake or before simulated-reader testing. ([Takramer/deep-redesign](https://github.com/Takramer/deep-redesign/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-researcher.md))
- **persona-review-orchestrator**: Runs a citizen/user persona panel over a piece of UX, copy, or content — samples real-distribution-grounded Korean personas from a catalog, dispatches each as an independent panelist, and synthesizes their reactions into one report. Use for "how would ordinary users react", user-perspective / usability / copy-tone review, or "/persona-review". A complement to code-reviewer/security-reviewer, NOT a replacement — it judges user experience, never code correctness or security. ([joymin5655/Agent](https://github.com/joymin5655/Agent/blob/HEAD/extras/agents/persona-review-orchestrator.md) · ★2)
- **persona-reviewer**: Reviews a diff or PR as a specific extracted reviewer persona. Given a path to a persona file (personas/&lt;login&gt;.md) and something to review, it produces review comments in that reviewer's voice and priorities. Use one invocation per persona to run a review panel (the reviewer:review command / Agent Reviewer skill). ([ivankuznetsov/agent-plugins](https://github.com/ivankuznetsov/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-reviewer/agents/persona-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **persona-reviewer**: Read-only dispatch target for team personas in review, analysis, or assessment mode. ([MotWakorb/ai-agent-dev-team](https://github.com/MotWakorb/ai-agent-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-reviewer.md) · ★2)

Trigger: launched by the sdd-orchestrator at the design and apply phase boundaries; not for general tasks. ([Keiron-HealthTech/spec-driven-dev](https://github.com/Keiron-HealthTech/spec-driven-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/phase-validator.md) · ★1)
- **phase-verifier**: Fresh-context acceptance verifier dispatched by /implementation:implement-dispatch at phase boundaries and for post-phase source commits: checks a phase's binary acceptance criteria against the actual diff with the orchestrator's rationale withheld, and returns a per-criterion verdict grounded in direct evidence. Its tool cage bars Edit/Write and agent spawning; Bash and PowerShell remain for inspection and, in an orchestrator-created throwaway worktree, for running holdout tests or re-running a check with edited test files restored from base. Not intended for direct ad-hoc use. ([melodic-software/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/melodic-software/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/implementation/agents/phase-verifier.md) · ★32)
- **phase-verifier**: Generic goal-backward phase verifier. Reads a phase dir's PLAN.md goal statement, checks the artifacts in the dir against it, writes verification.json, and emits VERIFICATION.md only on pass. ([Blazity/nextjs-migration-plugin](https://github.com/Blazity/nextjs-migration-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/phase-verifier.md) · ★2)
- **phase-verifier**: Verifies a finished change against the Definition of Done — reads CI on the PR's current head (it never runs a full suite locally), then walks the changed flow in a real browser against the PR's Coolify preview. Use when implementation is complete and you are about to claim it works. Reports evidence, never fixes. ([Neablis/travel-collab](https://github.com/Neablis/travel-collab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **phase-verifier**: Fresh-context check of the current phase's BRIEF.md exit criteria, with evidence for each. Use when a phase may be complete. ([santheep09/RoverSimulation](https://github.com/santheep09/RoverSimulation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **phase-verifier**: Phase-level verification agent. Runs integration tests defined by the phase assessor, verifies cross-phase wiring, and issues a Phase Verification Gate (PVG) token on pass. ([RhizomaticRobin/Gatekeeper](https://github.com/RhizomaticRobin/Gatekeeper/blob/HEAD/agents/phase-verifier.md))
- **phase-verifier**: 단계 작업이 끝났다고 할 때, 그 단계 명세(docs/prompts/PHASE-NN.md)의 인수 기준과 원칙·문서 갱신을 구현에 참여하지 않은 눈으로 하나씩 확인한다. 단계를 마치기 전에 반드시 use proactively. ([SEUNGMINSHIN7277/sangsang-playground](https://github.com/SEUNGMINSHIN7277/sangsang-playground/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-verifier.md))
- **phase-verifier**: Consolidates a roadmap phase's TDD results and one final full regression run when delegated. Reuses valid focused results; no mandatory fresh context, repeated replay or independent evidence-review loop. Writes a concise verification result without fixing source or changing phase status. ([vasiloglou/dev-tools](https://github.com/vasiloglou/dev-tools/blob/HEAD/agents/phase-verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **phase-verifier**: Independently verify a phase's Success Criteria from log files and tests. Use after phase-executor reports done, before moving on. ([ximxim/liquid-editor](https://github.com/ximxim/liquid-editor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-verifier.md))
- **phase-verify**: {{PHASE_DESCRIPTION}} ([magicpro97/vibeflow](https://github.com/magicpro97/vibeflow/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/phase-verify.md) · ★8)
- **phase-verify-agent**: Validates generated components for schema compliance. Returns validation results for main LLM to act on. ([chkim-su/forge2](https://github.com/chkim-su/forge2/blob/HEAD/agents/phase-verify-agent.md))
- **phase-worker**: Executes one roadmap phase end-to-end inside a driver-prepared git worktree on its auto/&lt;phase-id&gt; branch. Implements slices, runs the project's quality gate, commits per slice, renames the phase doc to DONE_. Never merges, never edits the manifest, never pushes. Spawned by the /pilot-run driver with the phase id, worktree path, and gate command in the task prompt. Project-agnostic - repo conventions come from the repo itself. ([jopnick/claudopilot](https://github.com/jopnick/claudopilot/blob/HEAD/pilot/agents/phase-worker.md) · ★4)
- **phase-worker**: Implements a development phase in an isolated git worktree, following CLAUDE.md conventions and the project plan. ([jreuben11/triad](https://github.com/jreuben11/triad/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-worker.md))

- **phase5_kickoff**: **Created**: 2025-11-20 **Updated**: 2025-04-22 (Expanded with architecture details, code references, and integration patterns) **Status**: Active **Purpose**: Provide comprehensive technical reference for Phase 5 parallel agent squad development, including architecture, coordination patterns, integration contracts, and execution workflows. **Related**: - `docs/PHASE5_PARALLEL_PLAN.md` (Master coo ([kivo360/OmoiOS](https://github.com/kivo360/OmoiOS/blob/HEAD/docs/.archive-20260422-0900/design/agents/phase5_kickoff.md) · ★80)
- **PHASE5_STEP1_AI_BACKEND**: 1. **AI kodu tamamen example_app/ içinde yaşar** — drawing_core ve drawing_ui'ya AI bağımlılığı eklenmez 2. **Tüm AI API çağrıları Supabase Edge Functions üzerinden geçer** — API key'ler asla client'ta olmaz 3. **Multi-model routing** — Task tipine ve user tier'ına göre farklı model seçilir 4. **Mevcut SubscriptionTier kullanılır** — `free → Gemini Flash`, `premium → GPT-4o-mini`, `premiumPlus → G ([aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool](https://github.com/aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/PHASE5_STEP1_AI_BACKEND.md) · ★1)
- **PHASE5_STEP2_CHAT_UI**: 1. **Canvas capture** → `canvasBoundaryKeyProvider` zaten mevcut (drawing_ui), capture service example_app'te olacak 2. **AI state** → Riverpod StateNotifier/AsyncNotifier pattern (mevcut proje tarzı) 3. **Chat UI** → Full-screen modal bottom sheet (telefon) veya side panel (tablet) — başlangıç olarak modal 4. **Streaming** → StreamBuilder ile token-by-token text append 5. **Markdown/LaTeX** → flu ([aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool](https://github.com/aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/PHASE5_STEP2_CHAT_UI.md) · ★1)
- **PHASE5_STEP3_LOCAL_STORAGE**: 1. **Mevcut `app_database.dart`'a AI tabloları ekle** — ayrı DB dosyası değil, çünkü projede zaten tek Drift DB pattern'ı var, schemaVersion artırılarak migration yapılacak 2. **Offline-first** — mesajlar önce lokal DB'ye yazılır, sonra Supabase'e sync edilir 3. **Conversation list** — AI chat modal'a geçmiş sohbet listesi sidebar/drawer eklenir 4. **Sync stratejisi** — Basit: kaydet → cloud'a gön ([aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool](https://github.com/aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/PHASE5_STEP3_LOCAL_STORAGE.md) · ★1)
- **PHASE5_STEP4_FREEMIUM**: 1. **Mevcut `subscriptionProvider` kullanılır** — yeni premium sistem oluşturulmaz 2. **AI provider'lar subscription'ı watch eder** — tier değişince otomatik güncellenir 3. **Upgrade prompt** — limit aşımında veya premium model istendiğinde gösterilir 4. **Usage bar** — AIChatModal header'ında günlük kullanım progress bar'ı 5. **Model badge** — Her AI yanıtında hangi modelin kullanıldığı gösterili ([aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool](https://github.com/aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/PHASE5_STEP4_FREEMIUM.md) · ★1)
